 let expected_sec = self.expected_seconds.unwrap_or(1800);\n87\t        if expected_sec > 0 {\n88\t            self.combined_score / expected_sec as f64\n89\t        } else {\n90\t            self.combined_score\n91\t        }\n92\t    }\n93\t}\n94\t\n95\timpl PartialOrd for Score {\n96\t    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<std::cmp::Ordering> {\n97\t impact per unit time is better)\n104\t        match self\n105\t            .velocity_adjusted_score()\n106\t            .partial_cmp(&other.velocity_adjusted_score())\n107\t        {\n108\t            Some(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal) => {}\n109\t            Some(ord) => return ord.reverse(),\n110\t            None => {\n111\t                // NaN comparison - treat as equal and fall through to Serialize)]\n136\tpub struct ScoredBead {\n137\t    pub id: String,\n138\t    pub title: String,\n139\t    pub status: String,\n140\t    pub priority: i32,\n141\t    pub downstream_impact: i64,\n142\t    pub critical_float: f64,\n143\t    pub created_at: String,\n144\t}\n145\t\n146\t/// Atomically claim a bead for a worker.\n147\t///\n148\t/// This function performs the following in a single IMMEDIATE transaction:\n149\t/// 1. Reclaim stale in_progress beads (older than claim_ttl_minutes) back to open\n150\t/// 2. Select candidates with downstream_impact + critical_float scoring\n151\t/// 3. Update the winner to in_progress with assignee=worker\n152\t/// 4. Insert an event\n153\t/// 5. Mark the bead as dirty\n154\t/// 6. TTL in minutes after which in_progress beads are reclaimed\n160\t/// * `worker_metadata` - Optional worker metadata (model, harness, version)\n161\t///\n162\t/// # Returns\n163\t/// * `Ok(Some(claim_result))` - A bead was claimed\n164\t/// * `Ok(None)` - No beads available to claim\n165\t/// * `Err(e)` - Transaction error\n166\tpub fn claim(\n167\t    tx: &Connection,\n168\t    worker: &str,\n169\t    claim_ttl_minutes: i64,\n170\t    now: DateTime<Utc>,\n171\t    worker_metadata: Option<&WorkerMetadata>,\n172\t) -> Result<Option<ClaimResult>> {\n173\t    // Step 0: Check migration_lock - return NONE if migration is in progress\n174\t    let lock_count: i64 = tx\n175\t        .query_row(\n176\t            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM migration_lock WHERE expires_at > ?1\",\n177\t            params![now.to_rfc3339()],\n178\t            |row| row.get(0),\n179\t        )\n180\t        .unwrap_or(0);\n181\t    if lock_count > 0 {\n182\t        // Migration in progress - i.priority) * 2.0\n241\t                 + 1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1)\n242\t             ) / COALESCE(vs.p50_seconds, 1800) DESC\n243\t             LIMIT 1\",\n244\t        )?;\n245\t\n246\t        let mut rows = stmt.query(params![m, h])?;\n247\t\n248\t        if let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n249\t            let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n250\t\n251\t            // Step 3: Update the winner to in_progress with a race condition check\n252\t            let rows_affected = tx.execute(\n253\t                \"UPDATE issues\n254\t                 SET status = 'in_progress', assignee = ?, updated_at = ?\n255\t                 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open'\",\n256\t                params![worker, now.to_rfc3339(), &bead_id],\n257\t            )?;\n258\t\n259\t            if rows_affected == 0 {\n260\t                return Ok(None);\n261\t            }\n262\t\n263\t            // Step 4: Record worker session if metadata provided\n264\t            if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n265\t                tx.execute(\n266\t                    \"INSERT INTO worker_sessions (worker_id, model, harness, harness_version, bead_id, workspace_path)\n267\t                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n268\t                    params![\n269\t                        &meta.worker_id,\n270\t                        meta.model.as_deref(),\n271\t                        meta.harness.as_deref(),\n272\t                        meta.harness_version.as_deref(),\n273\t                        &bead_id,\n274\t                        \"\",\n275\t                    ],\n276\t                )?;\n277\t            }\n278\t\n279\t            // Step 5: Insert event with worker metadata in comment field\n280\t            let metadata_json = worker_metadata.and_then(|m| serde_json::to_string(m).ok());\n281\t            tx.execute(\n282\t                \"INSERT INTO events (issue_id, event_type, actor, new_value, comment, created_at)\n283\t                 VALUES (?, 'claimed', ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n284\t                params![&bead_id, worker, worker, metadata_json, now.to_rfc3339()],\n285\t            )?;\n286\t\n287\t            // Step 6: Mark as dirty\n288\t            tx.execute(\n289\t                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dirty_issues (issue_id, marked_at)\n290\t                 VALUES (?, ?)\",\n291\t                params![&bead_id, now.to_rfc3339()],\n292\t            )?;\n293\t\n294\t            return Ok(Some(ClaimResult {\n295\t                bead_id,\n296\t                reclaimed,\n297\t                workspace_path: None,\n298\t            }));\n299\t        }\n300\t\n301\t        Ok(None)\n302\t    } else {\n303\t        // Standard scoring without velocity data (original SQL-based scoring)\n304\t        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n305\t            \"SELECT i.id, i.issue_type,\n306\t                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n307\t                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n308\t                    i.priority\n309\t             FROM issues i\n310\t             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n311\t             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n312\t             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n313\t               AND i.id\n321\t                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n322\t                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n323\t               )\n324\t             GROUP BY i.id\n325\t             ORDER BY\n326\t                 downstream_impact DESC,\n327\t                 critical_path_bonus DESC,\n328\t                 i.priority {\n336\t            let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n337\t\n338\t            // Step 3: Update the winner to in_progress with a race condition check\n339\t            // The WHERE status = 'open' condition ensures we only claim if still open\n340\t            let rows_affected = tx.execute(\n341\t                \"UPDATE issues\n342\t                 SET status = 'in_progress', assignee = ?, bead_id,\n385\t                reclaimed,\n386\t                workspace_path: None,\n387\t            }))\n388\t        } else {\n389\t            Ok(None)\n390\t        }\n391\t    }\n392\t}\n393\t\n394\t/// Get ready candidates using the same scoring logic as claim().\n395\t///\n396\t/// This returns a list of beads that would be considered for claiming,\n397\t/// ordered by the same scoring formula:\n398\t/// - velocity_adjusted_score DESC (impact per unit time, when velocity data available)\n399\t/// - downstream_impact DESC (more blocking = higher priority)\n400\t/// - critical_path_bonus DESC (1000.0/(float+1), higher bonus = more critical)\n401\t/// - priority ASC (0=Critical, 4=Backlog)\n402\t/// - created_at ASC (FIFO tiebreaker)\n403\t///\n404\t/// # Arguments\n405\t/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n406\t/// * `limit` - Maximum number of candidates to return\n407\t/// * `model` - Optional model name for velocity-aware scoring\n408\t/// * `harness` - Optional harness name for velocity-aware scoring\n409\t///\n410\t/// # Returns\n411\t/// * `Ok(Vec<ScoredBead>)` - List of scored bead candidates\n412\tpub fn get_ready_candidates(\n413\t    tx: &Connection,\n414\t    limit: usize,\n415\t    model: Option<&str>,\n416\t    harness: Option<&str>,\n417\t) -> Result<Vec<ScoredBead>> {\n418\t    let mut stmt = if let (Some(_m), Some(_h)) = (model, harness) {\n419\t        // Velocity-aware scoring: divide combined score by expected seconds\n420\t        tx.prepare(\n421\t            \"SELECT i.id, i.title, i.status, i.priority,\n422\t                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n423\t                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n424\t                    i.created_at,\n425\t                    vs.p50_seconds as expected_seconds\n426\t             FROM issues i\n427\t             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n428\t             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n429\t             LEFT JOIN velocity_stats vs ON vs.issue_type = i.issue_type\n430\t                 AND vs.model = ?1\n431\t                 AND vs.harness = ?2\n432\t             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n433\t               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n434\t               AND i.pinned = 0\n435\t               AND i.is_template = 0\n436\t               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n437\t               AND NOT EXISTS (\n438\t                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n439\t                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n440\t                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n441\t                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n442\t                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n443\t               )\n444\t             GROUP BY i.id\n445\t             ORDER BY (\n446\t                 COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) * 3.0\n447\t                 + (4 - i.priority) * 2.0\n448\t                 + 1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1)\n449\t             ) / COALESCE(vs.p50_seconds, 1800) DESC,\n450\t                 downstream_impact DESC,\n451\t                 critical_path_bonus DESC,\n452\t                 i.priority ASC,\n453\t                 i.created_at ASC\n454\t             LIMIT ?3\",\n455\t        )?\n456\t    } else {\n457\t        // Standard scoring without velocity data\n458\t        tx.prepare(\n459\t            \"SELECT i.id, i.title, i.status, i.priority,\n460\t                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n461\t                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n462\t                    i.created_at\n463\t             FROM issues i\n464\t             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n465\t             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n466\t             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n467\t               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n468\t               AND i.pinned = 0\n469\t               AND i.is_template = 0\n470\t               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n471\t               AND NOT EXISTS (\n472\t                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n473\t                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n474\t                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n475\t                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n476\t                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n477\t               )\n478\t             GROUP BY i.id\n479\t             ORDER BY\n480\t                 downstream_impact DESC,\n481\t                 critical_path_bonus DESC,\n482\t                 i.priority ASC,\n483\t                 i.created_at ASC\n484\t             LIMIT ?1\",\n485\t        )?\n486\t    };\n487\t\n488\t    let mut rows = if model.is_some() && harness.is_some() {\n489\t        stmt.query(params![model.unwrap(), harness.unwrap(), limit as i64])?\n490\t    } else {\n491\t        stmt.query(params![limit as i64])?\n492\t    };\n493\t\n494\t    let mut candidates = Vec::new();\n495\t\n496\t    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n497\t        candidates.push(ScoredBead {\n498\t            id: row.get(0)?,\n499\t            title: row.get(1)?,\n500\t            status: row.get(2)?,\n501\t            priority: row.get(3)?,\n502\t            downstream_impact: row.get(4)?,\n503\t            critical_float: row.get(5)?,\n504\t            created_at: row.get(6)?,\n505\t        });\n506\t    }\n507\t\n508\t    Ok(candidates)\n509\t}\n510\t\n511\t/// Claim from the highest-priority bead across multiple workspaces.\n512\t///\n513\t/// Scores each workspace's top candidate, picks the global winner,\n514\t/// and claims from that workspace.\n515\t///\n516\t/// # Arguments\n517\t/// * `workspace_paths` - Slice of workspace directory paths\n518\t/// * `worker` - The worker ID claiming the bead\n519\t/// * `claim_ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes after which in_progress beads are reclaimed\n520\t/// * `worker_metadata` - Optional worker metadata (model, harness, version)\n521\t///\n522\t/// # Returns\n523\t/// * `Ok(Some(claim_result))` - A bead was claimed (with workspace_path set)\n524\t/// * `Ok(None)` - No beads available to claim in any workspace\n525\t/// * `Err(e)` - Transaction error\n526\tpub fn claim_any(\n527\t    workspace_paths: &[PathBuf],\n528\t    worker: &str,\n529\t    claim_ttl_minutes: i64,\n530\t    worker_metadata: Option<&WorkerMetadata>,\n531\t) -> Result<Option<ClaimResult>> {\n532\t    use crate::config::load_metadata;\n533\t    use crate::storage::Storage;\n534\t\n535\t    // Extract model and harness for velocity-aware scoring\n536\t    let (model, harness) = if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n537\t        (meta.model.as_deref(), meta.harness.as_deref())\n538\t    } else {\n539\t        (None, None)\n540\t    };\n541\t\n542\t    // Score across all workspaces\n543\t    let mut best: Option<(Score, usize)> = None;\n544\t    for (idx, workspace_path) in workspace_paths.iter().enumerate() {\n545\t        let beads_dir = get_beads_dir(workspace_path)?;\n546\t        let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n547\t        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n548\t\n549\t        // Open each workspace's SQLite\n550\t        match Storage::open(&db_path) {\n551\t            Ok(storage) => {\n552\t                if let Some(score) = storage.top_candidate_score(model, harness)? {\n553\t                    if best.as_ref().map(|(b, _)| score > *b).unwrap_or(true) {\n554\t                        best = Some((score, idx));\n555\t                    }\n556\t                }\n557\t            }\n558\t            Err(_) => {\n559\t                // Skip workspaces that can't be opened (e.g., no .beads directory)\n560\t                continue;\n561\t            }\n562\t        }\n563\t    }\n564\t\n565\t    match best {\n566\t        None => Ok(None),\n567\t        Some((_, workspace_idx)) => {\n568\t            let workspace_path = &workspace_paths[workspace_idx];\n569\t            let beads_dir = get_beads_dir(workspace_path)?;\n570\t            let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n571\t            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n572\t            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n573\t\n574\t            let now = Utc::now();\n575\t            match storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n576\t                claim(tx, worker, claim_ttl_minutes, now, worker_metadata)\n577\t            })? {\n578\t                Some(mut result) => {\n579\t                    result.workspace_path = Some(workspace_path.clone());\n580\t                    Ok(Some(result))\n581\t                }\n582\t                None => Ok(None),\n583\t            }\n584\t        }\n585\t    }\n586\t}\n587\t\n588\t/// Get the .beads directory from a workspace path.\n589\t///\n590\t/// If the workspace path itself contains a .beads directory, use it.\n591\t/// Otherwise, assume the path IS the .beads directory.\n592\tfn get_beads_dir(workspace_path: &Path) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf> {\n593\t    let beads_dir = workspace_path.join(\".beads\");\n594\t    if beads_dir.is_dir() {\n595\t        Ok(beads_dir)\n596\t    } else if workspace_path.ends_with(\".beads\") {\n597\t        Ok(workspace_path.to_path_buf())\n598\t    } else {\n599\t        use anyhow::bail;\n600\t        bail!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace_path)\n601\t    }\n602\t}\n603\t\n604\t/// Find all bead workspace directories starting from a search path.\n605\t///\n606\t/// Searches for directories containing a .beads subdirectory.\n607\t/// Searches upward from the start path through parent directories.\n608\tpub fn find_workspaces(start_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {\n609\t    let mut workspaces = Vec::new();\n610\t\n611\t    // Start from the given path and search upward\n612\t    let mut current = start_path.to_path_buf();\n613\t    loop {\n614\t        let beads_dir = current.join(\".beads\");\n615\t        if beads_dir.is_dir() {\n616\t            // Found a workspace - add the parent directory\n617\t            workspaces.push(current.clone());\n618\t        }\n619\t\n620\t        // Move to parent directory\n621\t        if !current.pop() {\n622\t            // Reached the root, stop searching\n623\t            break;\n624\t        }\n625\t    }\n626\t\n627\t    Ok(workspaces)\n628\t}\n629\t\n630\t#[cfg(test)]\n631\tmod tests {\n632\t    use super::*;\n633\t );\n795\t    }\n796\t\n797\t    #[test]\n798\t    fn test_critical_path_bonus_in_claim() {\n799\t        use crate::storage::schema;\n800\t\n801\t        let (_temp, mut storage) = model/harness claimed a bead\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\npub struct WorkerMetadata {\n    pub worker_id: String,\n    pub model: Option<String>,\n    pub harness: Option<String>,\n    pub harness_version: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Result of a claim operation\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct ClaimResult {\n    pub bead_id: String,\n    pub reclaimed: usize,\n    pub workspace_path: Option<PathBuf>,\n}\n\n/// Score for cross-workspace candidate comparison.\n///\n/// Higher scores are better. Ordered by:\n/// 1. combined_score / expected_seconds (impact per unit time, when velocity data available)\n/// 2. downstream_impact (more blocking = higher priority)\n/// 3. negative critical_float (lower float = more critical)\n/// 4. negative priority (lower number = higher priority)\n/// 5. negative created timestamp (older = higher priority/FIFO)\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\npub struct Score {\n    pub downstream_impact: i64,\n    pub critical_float: i64,\n    pub priority: i32,\n    pub created_at_ts: i64,\n    /// Expected duration in seconds from velocity_stats (None = no velocity data)\n    pub expected_seconds: Option<i64>,\n    /// Combined impact score (downstream * 3 + (4-priority) * 2 + critical_bonus)\n    pub combined_score: f64,\n}\n\nimpl PartialEq for Score {\n    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {\n        self.downstream_impact == other.downstream_impact\n            && self.critical_float == other.critical_float\n            && self.priority == other.priority\n            && self.created_at_ts == other.created_at_ts\n            && self.expected_seconds == other.expected_seconds\n            && self\n                .combined_score\n                .partial_cmp(&other.combined_score)\n                .map(|o| o == std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)\n                .unwrap_or(false)\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Eq for Score {}\n\nimpl Score {\n    /// Create a new score from candidate fields.\n    pub fn new(\n        downstream_impact: i64,\n        critical_float: i64,\n        priority: i32,\n        created_at_ts: i64,\n        expected_seconds: Option<i64>,\n        combined_score: f64,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            downstream_impact,\n            critical_float,\n            priority,\n            created_at_ts,\n            expected_seconds,\n            combined_score,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Get the velocity-adjusted score (impact per unit time).\n    ///\n    /// When expected_seconds is available, returns combined_score / expected_seconds.\n    /// Otherwise returns combined_score (no adjustment).\n    fn velocity_adjusted_score(&self) -> f64 {\n        let expected_sec = self.expected_seconds.unwrap_or(1800);\n        if expected_sec > 0 {\n            self.combined_score / expected_sec as f64\n        } else {\n            self.combined_score\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl PartialOrd for Score {\n    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<std::cmp::Ordering> {\n        Some(self.cmp(other))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Ord for Score {\n    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> std::cmp::Ordering {\n        // Primary: velocity-adjusted score DESC (higher impact per unit time is better)\n        match self\n            .velocity_adjusted_score()\n            .partial_cmp(&other.velocity_adjusted_score())\n        {\n            Some(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal) => {}\n            Some(ord) => return ord.reverse(),\n            None => {\n                // NaN comparison - treat as equal and fall through to other fields\n            }\n        }\n        // downstream_impact: DESC (higher is better)\n        match other.downstream_impact.cmp(&self.downstream_impact) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // critical_float: ASC (lower is better)\n        match self.critical_float.cmp(&other.critical_float) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // priority: ASC (lower number is better)\n        match self.priority.cmp(&other.priority) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // created_at_ts: ASC (older is better/FIFO)\n        self.created_at_ts.cmp(&other.created_at_ts)\n    }\n}\n\n/// A bead with its score for ready/claim operations\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\npub struct ScoredBead {\n    pub id: String,\n    pub title: String,\n    pub status: String,\n    pub priority: i32,\n    pub downstream_impact: i64,\n    pub critical_float: f64,\n    pub created_at: String,\n}\n\n/// Atomically claim a bead for a worker.\n///\n/// This function performs the following in a single IMMEDIATE transaction:\n/// 1. Reclaim stale in_progress beads (older than claim_ttl_minutes) back to open\n/// 2. Select candidates with downstream_impact + critical_float scoring\n/// 3. Update the winner to in_progress with assignee=worker\n/// 4. Insert an event\n/// 5. Mark the bead as dirty\n/// 6. Commit\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use (must be an IMMEDIATE transaction)\n/// * `worker` - The worker ID claiming the bead\n/// * `claim_ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes after which in_progress beads are reclaimed\n/// * `worker_metadata` - Optional worker metadata (model, harness, version)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Some(claim_result))` - A bead was claimed\n/// * `Ok(None)` - No beads available to claim\n/// * `Err(e)` - Transaction error\npub fn claim(\n    tx: &Connection,\n    worker: &str,\n    claim_ttl_minutes: i64,\n    now: DateTime<Utc>,\n    worker_metadata: Option<&WorkerMetadata>,\n) -> Result<Option<ClaimResult>> {\n    // Step 0: Check migration_lock - return NONE if migration is in progress\n    let lock_count: i64 = tx\n        .query_row(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM migration_lock WHERE expires_at > ?1\",\n            params![now.to_rfc3339()],\n            |row| row.get(0),\n        )\n        .unwrap_or(0);\n    if lock_count > 0 {\n        // Migration in progress - return None gracefully\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n\n    // Step 1: Reclaim stale in_progress beads\n    let stale_cutoff = now - Duration::minutes(claim_ttl_minutes);\n    let reclaimed = tx.execute(\n        \"UPDATE issues\n         SET status = 'open', assignee = NULL, updated_at = ?\n         WHERE status = 'in_progress'\n           AND updated_at < ?\",\n        params![now.to_rfc3339(), stale_cutoff.to_rfc3339()],\n    )?;\n\n    // Step 2: Find candidate beads with impact scoring\n    // Score = downstream_impact + critical_path_bonus\n    // downstream_impact = count of beads blocked by this one\n    // critical_path_bonus = 1000.0 / (float + 1) where float is from critical_path_cache\n    // Zero-float beads get bonus = 1000, float-5 beads get ~167, non-critical beads get ~1\n    // Velocity-aware scoring: prefer beads with lower expected duration\n    // score = impact / expected_seconds (with fallback to standard scoring)\n    let (model, harness) = if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n        (meta.model.clone(), meta.harness.clone())\n    } else {\n        (None, None)\n    };\n\n    // Velocity-aware claim: JOIN velocity_stats for requesting worker.\n    // score = (impact * 3.0 + (4 - priority) * 2.0 + critical_path_bonus) / p50_seconds\n    // Fallback p50_seconds = 1800 when no velocity data exists for this (model, harness, issue_type).\n    if model.is_some() && harness.is_some() {\n        let m = model.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        let h = harness.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\");\n\n        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT i.id\n             FROM issues i\n             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id\n                 AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n             LEFT JOIN velocity_stats vs ON vs.issue_type = i.issue_type\n                 AND vs.model = ?1\n                 AND vs.harness = ?2\n             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n               AND i.pinned = 0\n               AND i.is_template = 0\n               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n               AND NOT EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n               )\n             GROUP BY i.id\n             ORDER BY (\n                 COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) * 3.0\n                 + (4 - i.priority) * 2.0\n                 + 1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1)\n             ) / COALESCE(vs.p50_seconds, 1800) DESC\n             LIMIT 1\",\n        )?;\n\n        let mut rows = stmt.query(params![m, h])?;\n\n        if let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n            let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n\n            // Step 3: Update the winner to in_progress with a race condition check\n            let rows_affected = tx.execute(\n                \"UPDATE issues\n                 SET status = 'in_progress', assignee = ?, updated_at = ?\n                 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open'\",\n                params![worker, now.to_rfc3339(), &bead_id],\n            )?;\n\n            if rows_affected == 0 {\n                return Ok(None);\n            }\n\n            // Step 4: Record worker session if metadata provided\n            if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO worker_sessions (worker_id, model, harness, harness_version, bead_id, workspace_path)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        &meta.worker_id,\n                        meta.model.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness_version.as_deref(),\n                        &bead_id,\n                        \"\",\n                    ],\n                )?;\n            }\n\n            // Step 5: Insert event with worker metadata in comment field\n            let metadata_json = worker_metadata.and_then(|m| serde_json::to_string(m).ok());\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO events (issue_id, event_type, actor, new_value, comment, created_at)\n                 VALUES (?, 'claimed', ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, worker, worker, metadata_json, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            // Step 6: Mark as dirty\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dirty_issues (issue_id, marked_at)\n                 VALUES (?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            return Ok(Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id,\n                reclaimed,\n                workspace_path: None,\n            }));\n        }\n\n        Ok(None)\n    } else {\n        // Standard scoring without velocity data (original SQL-based scoring)\n        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT i.id, i.issue_type,\n                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n                    i.priority\n             FROM issues i\n             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n               AND i.pinned = 0\n               AND i.is_template = 0\n               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n               AND NOT EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n               )\n             GROUP BY i.id\n             ORDER BY\n                 downstream_impact DESC,\n                 critical_path_bonus DESC,\n                 i.priority ASC,\n                 i.created_at ASC\n             LIMIT 1\",\n        )?;\n\n        let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n\n        if let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n            let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n\n            // Step 3: Update the winner to in_progress with a race condition check\n            // The WHERE status = 'open' condition ensures we only claim if still open\n            let rows_affected = tx.execute(\n                \"UPDATE issues\n                 SET status = 'in_progress', assignee = ?, updated_at = ?\n                 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open'\",\n                params![worker, now.to_rfc3339(), &bead_id],\n            )?;\n\n            // If no rows were affected, another worker claimed this bead first\n            if rows_affected == 0 {\n                return Ok(None);\n            }\n\n            // Step 4: Record worker session if metadata provided\n            if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO worker_sessions (worker_id, model, harness, harness_version, bead_id, workspace_path)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        &meta.worker_id,\n                        meta.model.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness_version.as_deref(),\n                        &bead_id,\n                        \"\", // workspace_path not available in transaction context\n                    ],\n                )?;\n            }\n\n            // Step 5: Insert event with worker metadata in comment field\n            let metadata_json = worker_metadata.and_then(|m| serde_json::to_string(m).ok());\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO events (issue_id, event_type, actor, new_value, comment, created_at)\n                 VALUES (?, 'claimed', ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, worker, worker, metadata_json, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            // Step 6: Mark as dirty\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dirty_issues (issue_id, marked_at)\n                 VALUES (?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            Ok(Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id,\n                reclaimed,\n                workspace_path: None,\n            }))\n        } else {\n            Ok(None)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Get ready candidates using the same scoring logic as claim().\n///\n/// This returns a list of beads that would be considered for claiming,\n/// ordered by the same scoring formula:\n/// - velocity_adjusted_score DESC (impact per unit time, when velocity data available)\n/// - downstream_impact DESC (more blocking = higher priority)\n/// - critical_path_bonus DESC (1000.0/(float+1), higher bonus = more critical)\n/// - priority ASC (0=Critical, 4=Backlog)\n/// - created_at ASC (FIFO tiebreaker)\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n/// * `limit` - Maximum number of candidates to return\n/// * `model` - Optional model name for velocity-aware scoring\n/// * `harness` - Optional harness name for velocity-aware scoring\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Vec<ScoredBead>)` - List of scored bead candidates\npub fn get_ready_candidates(\n    tx: &Connection,\n    limit: usize,\n    model: Option<&str>,\n    harness: Option<&str>,\n) -> Result<Vec<ScoredBead>> {\n    let mut stmt = if let (Some(_m), Some(_h)) = (model, harness) {\n        // Velocity-aware scoring: divide combined score by expected seconds\n        tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT i.id, i.title, i.status, i.priority,\n                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n                    i.created_at,\n                    vs.p50_seconds as expected_seconds\n             FROM issues i\n             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n             LEFT JOIN velocity_stats vs ON vs.issue_type = i.issue_type\n                 AND vs.model = ?1\n                 AND vs.harness = ?2\n             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n               AND i.pinned = 0\n               AND i.is_template = 0\n               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n               AND NOT EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n               )\n             GROUP BY i.id\n             ORDER BY (\n                 COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) * 3.0\n                 + (4 - i.priority) * 2.0\n                 + 1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1)\n             ) / COALESCE(vs.p50_seconds, 1800) DESC,\n                 downstream_impact DESC,\n                 critical_path_bonus DESC,\n                 i.priority ASC,\n                 i.created_at ASC\n             LIMIT ?3\",\n        )?\n    } else {\n        // Standard scoring without velocity data\n        tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT i.id, i.title, i.status, i.priority,\n                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n                    i.created_at\n             FROM issues i\n             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n               AND i.pinned = 0\n               AND i.is_template = 0\n               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n               AND NOT EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n               )\n             GROUP BY i.id\n             ORDER BY\n                 downstream_impact DESC,\n                 critical_path_bonus DESC,\n                 i.priority ASC,\n                 i.created_at ASC\n             LIMIT ?1\",\n        )?\n    };\n\n    let mut rows = if model.is_some() && harness.is_some() {\n        stmt.query(params![model.unwrap(), harness.unwrap(), limit as i64])?\n    } else {\n        stmt.query(params![limit as i64])?\n    };\n\n    let mut candidates = Vec::new();\n\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n        candidates.push(ScoredBead {\n            id: row.get(0)?,\n            title: row.get(1)?,\n            status: row.get(2)?,\n            priority: row.get(3)?,\n            downstream_impact: row.get(4)?,\n            critical_float: row.get(5)?,\n            created_at: row.get(6)?,\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(candidates)\n}\n\n/// Claim from the highest-priority bead across multiple workspaces.\n///\n/// Scores each workspace's top candidate, picks the global winner,\n/// and claims from that workspace.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_paths` - Slice of workspace directory paths\n/// * `worker` - The worker ID claiming the bead\n/// * `claim_ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes after which in_progress beads are reclaimed\n/// * `worker_metadata` - Optional worker metadata (model, harness, version)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Some(claim_result))` - A bead was claimed (with workspace_path set)\n/// * `Ok(None)` - No beads available to claim in any workspace\n/// * `Err(e)` - Transaction error\npub fn claim_any(\n    workspace_paths: &[PathBuf],\n    worker: &str,\n    claim_ttl_minutes: i64,\n    worker_metadata: Option<&WorkerMetadata>,\n) -> Result<Option<ClaimResult>> {\n    use crate::config::load_metadata;\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n\n    // Extract model and harness for velocity-aware scoring\n    let (model, harness) = if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n        (meta.model.as_deref(), meta.harness.as_deref())\n    } else {\n        (None, None)\n    };\n\n    // Score across all workspaces\n    let mut best: Option<(Score, usize)> = None;\n    for (idx, workspace_path) in workspace_paths.iter().enumerate() {\n        let beads_dir = get_beads_dir(workspace_path)?;\n        let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n        // Open each workspace's SQLite\n        match Storage::open(&db_path) {\n            Ok(storage) => {\n                if let Some(score) = storage.top_candidate_score(model, harness)? {\n                    if best.as_ref().map(|(b, _)| score > *b).unwrap_or(true) {\n                        best = Some((score, idx));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                // Skip workspaces that can't be opened (e.g., no .beads directory)\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    match best {\n        None => Ok(None),\n        Some((_, workspace_idx)) => {\n            let workspace_path = &workspace_paths[workspace_idx];\n            let beads_dir = get_beads_dir(workspace_path)?;\n            let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n            let now = Utc::now();\n            match storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                claim(tx, worker, claim_ttl_minutes, now, worker_metadata)\n            })? {\n                Some(mut result) => {\n                    result.workspace_path = Some(workspace_path.clone());\n                    Ok(Some(result))\n                }\n                None => Ok(None),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Get the .beads directory from a workspace path.\n///\n/// If the workspace path itself contains a .beads directory, use it.\n/// Otherwise, assume the path IS the .beads directory.\nfn get_beads_dir(workspace_path: &Path) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf> {\n    let beads_dir = workspace_path.join(\".beads\");\n    if beads_dir.is_dir() {\n        Ok(beads_dir)\n    } else if workspace_path.ends_with(\".beads\") {\n        Ok(workspace_path.to_path_buf())\n    } else {\n        use anyhow::bail;\n        bail!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace_path)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Find all bead workspace directories starting from a search path.\n///\n/// Searches for directories containing a .beads subdirectory.\n/// Searches upward from the start path through parent directories.\npub fn find_workspaces(start_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {\n    let mut workspaces = Vec::new();\n\n    // Start from the given path and search upward\n    let mut current = start_path.to_path_buf();\n    loop {\n        let beads_dir = current.join(\".beads\");\n        if beads_dir.is_dir() {\n            // Found a workspace - add the parent directory\n            workspaces.push(current.clone());\n        }\n\n        // Move to parent directory\n        if !current.pop() {\n            // Reached the root, stop searching\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(workspaces)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::model::{Issue, Status};\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n\n    fn setup_test_db() -> (tempfile::NamedTempFile, Storage) {\n        let temp_file = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();\n        let storage = Storage::open(temp_file.path()).unwrap();\n        (temp_file, storage)\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_basic() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create an open bead\n        let issue = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-test1\".to_string(),\n            \"Test bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n\n        // Claim it\n        let result = storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| claim(tx, \"worker1\", 30, Utc::now(), None))\n            .unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_some());\n        let claim_result = result.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.bead_id, \"bf-test1\");\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.reclaimed, 0);\n\n        // Verify the bead is now in_progress\n        let updated = storage.get_issue(\"bf-test1\").unwrap().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(updated.status, Status::InProgress);\n        assert_eq!(updated.assignee.as_ref().unwrap(), \"worker1\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_no_candidates() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // No beads available\n        let result = storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| claim(tx, \"worker1\", 30, Utc::now(), None))\n            .unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_reclaims_stale() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create an in_progress bead with old updated_at\n        let mut issue = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-stale\".to_string(),\n            \"Stale bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        issue.status = Status::InProgress;\n        issue.assignee = Some(\"worker_old\".to_string());\n        issue.updated_at = Utc::now() - Duration::minutes(60);\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n\n        // Create an open bead (slightly newer so bf-stale should be claimed first after reclaim)\n        let issue2 = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-open\".to_string(),\n            \"Open bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        storage.create_issue(&issue2).unwrap();\n\n        // Claim with 30 min TTL - should reclaim the stale one\n        let result = storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| claim(tx, \"worker_new\", 30, Utc::now(), None))\n            .unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_some());\n        let claim_result = result.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.reclaimed, 1);\n\n        // The stale bead should be claimed (it was reclaimed to open, then claimed since it's older)\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.bead_id, \"bf-stale\");\n\n        // Verify the claimed bead is now owned by worker_new\n        let claimed = storage.get_issue(\"bf-stale\").unwrap().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(claimed.status, Status::InProgress);\n        assert_eq!(claimed.assignee.as_ref().unwrap(), \"worker_new\");\n\n        // The open bead should still be open\n        let still_open = storage.get_issue(\"bf-open\").unwrap().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(still_open.status, Status::Open);\n        assert!(still_open.assignee.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_concurrent_claim_no_double_claim() {\n        use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\n        use std::thread;\n\n        let (_temp, storage) = setup_test_db();\n        let storage = Arc::new(storage);\n\n        // Create 20 open beads\n        for i in 0..20 {\n            let issue = Issue::new(\n                format!(\"bf-{:0>4}\", i),\n                format!(\"Test bead {}\", i),\n                \".\".to_string(),\n            );\n            storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n        }\n\n        // Spawn 20 workers trying to claim concurrently\n        let claimed_beads: Arc<Mutex<Vec<String>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let mut handles = vec![];\n\n        for worker_id in 0..20 {\n            let storage_clone = Arc::clone(&storage);\n            let claimed_clone = Arc::clone(&claimed_beads);\n\n            let handle = thread::spawn(move || {\n                let result = storage_clone\n                    .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                        claim(tx, &format!(\"worker-{}\", worker_id), 30, Utc::now(), None)\n                    })\n                    .unwrap();\n\n });\n\n            handles.push(handle);\n        }\n\n        // Wait for all workers to complete\n        for handle in handles {\n            handle.join().unwrap();\n        }\n\n        let claimed = claimed_beads.lock().unwrap();\n\n        // All 20 beads should be claimed exactly once\n        assert_eq!(\n            claimed.len(),\n            20,\n            \"Expected 20 unique claims, got {}\",\n            claimed.len()\n        );\n\n        // No duplicates allowed\n        let mut unique_beads = claimed.clone();\n        unique_beads.sort();\n        unique_beads.dedup();\n        assert_eq!(\n            unique_beads.len(),\n            20,\n            \"Found duplicate claims: {:?}\",\n            claimed\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_critical_path_bonus_in_claim() {\n        use crate::storage::schema;\n\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create a dependency chain: bf-critical (float=0) -> bf-blocked\n        // And an independent bead: bf-independent (no critical path data)\n\n        let critical = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-critical\".to_string(),\n            \"Critical path bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        storage.create_issue(&critical).unwrap();\n\n        let blocked = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-blocked\".to_string(),\n dependency: bf-blocked depends on bf-critical\n        storage\n            .add_dependency(\n                \"bf-blocked\",\n                \"bf-critical\",\n                &crate::model::DependencyType::Blocks,\n                \"test\",\n            )\n            .unwrap();\n\n        // Manually populate critical_path_cache\n        // (create_issue already calls compute_all_critical_paths, so use REPLACE)\n        storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                // First, clear any existing entries for our test beads\n                tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM critical_path_cache WHERE bead_id IN ('bf-critical', 'bf-blocked', 'bf-independent')\", [])?;\n\n                // bf-critical has float=0 (on critical path)\n params![\n                        \"bf-critical\",\n                        \"bf-critical\",\n                        0i64,  // es\n                        0i64,  // ls\n                        0i64,  // float = ls - es = 0\n                        Utc::now().to_rfc3339()\n                    ],\n                )?;\n\n                // bf-blocked has float=1 (not on critical path)\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO critical_path_cache (bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float, updated_at)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        \"bf-blocked\",\n                        \"bf-critical\",\n                        1i64,  // es\n                        2i64,  // ls\n                        1i64,  // float = ls - es = 1\n                        Utc::now().to_rfc3339()\n                    ],\n                )?;\n\n                // bf-independent has no entry in critical_path_cache\n                Ok(())\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n\n        // Get ready candidates - should be ordered by critical path bonus\n        let candidates = storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| get_ready_candidates(tx, 10, None, None))\n            .unwrap();\n\n        // bf-critical should be first (bonus=1000.0/1=1000)\n        // bf-independent should be second (bonus=1000.0/1000=1)\n        // bf-blocked is NOT in the list because it's blocked by bf-critical\n        assert_eq!(candidates.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(candidates[0].id, \"bf-critical\");\n        assert_eq!(candidates[1].id, \"bf-independent\");\n\n        // Verify bonus values\n        // critical_float stores the BONUS value, not the raw float\n        assert!((candidates[0].critical_float - 1000.0).abs() < 0.01); // float=0 → bonus=1000\n        assert!((candidates[1].critical_float - 1.0).abs() < 0.01); // no cache → bonus≈1\n    }\n\n {\n348\t    // CLI-level integration test for `bf claim --fallback any`.\n349\t    // Creates two workspaces, exhausts workspace A, verifies that\n350\t    // `bf claim --workspace A --fallback any` returns a bead from workspace B.\n351\t\n352\t    use std::process::Command;\n353\t    use std::path::PathBuf;\n354\t\n355\t    let workspace_a = common::TempWorkspace::new().unwrap();\n356\t    let workspace_b = common::TempWorkspace::new().unwrap();\n357\t\n358\t    // Workspace A: no beads (exhausted)\n359\t    // Workspace B: 2 beads available\n360\t    workspace_b.create_bead(\"bf-b1\", \"Bead in B\").unwrap();\n361\t    workspace_b.create_bead(\"bf-b2\", \"Another bead in B\").unwrap();\n362\t\n363\t    // Build the bf binary if it doesn't exist\n364\t    // Note: cargo tests run with cwd = project root, but we change current_dir for the command\n365\t    // so we need an absolute path to the binary\n366\t    let project_root = std::env::current_dir().unwrap();\n367\t    let bf_binary: PathBuf = if cfg!(debug_assertions) {\n368\t        project_root.join(\"target/debug/bf\")\n369\t    } else {\n370\t        project_root.join(\"target/release/bf\")\n371\t let ws = common::TempWorkspace::new().unwrap();\n\n    // Create beads with varying priorities\n    for i in 0..num_beads {\n        let mut bead = bead_forge::Issue::new(\n            format!(\"bf-{:0>4}\", i),\n            format!(\"Test bead {}\", i),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        bead.priority = bead_forge::model::Priority((i % 5) as i32); // Vary priority 0-4\n        ws.create_bead(&bead.id, &bead.title).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    let storage = Arc::new(ws.storage().unwrap());\n    let claimed_ids = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n    let let result = storage_clone.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                bead_forge::claim::claim(\n                    tx,\n                    &format!(\"worker-{:02}\", worker_id),\n                    30,\n                    Utc::now(),\n                    None,\n                )\n            });\n\n            match result {\n                Ok(Some(claimed)) => {\n panic!(\"Worker errors occurred: {:?}\", worker_errors);\n    }\n\n    // Verify no duplicates\n    let mut unique_ids = std::collections::HashSet::new();\n    let mut duplicates = Vec::new();\n\n    for id in claimed_ids.iter() {\n        if !unique_ids.insert(id) {\n            duplicates.push(id.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    if !duplicates.is_empty() {\n        panic!(\n
